# PrestasiPlus Rebuild — Project Tracker

> Reverse-engineering `https://prestasiplus.my` (excluding the public landing page) into a new system.
> Target FE: **Vue 3 + Vite + Tailwind CSS + Lucide**.
> **Current scope: complete front-end prototype with dummy data** — every module's UI and flows clickable end-to-end, covered by Playwright E2E tests. **Backend is out of scope for now** (later phase).
> Current phase: **G1 — Requirements (URS & SRS), module by module.**

### Scope decisions
| # | Decision | Date |
|---|---|---|
| D1 | Deliverable = FE prototype + dummy data + E2E tests for all 26 modules. No real backend. | 2026-09-25 |
| D2 | Prototype talks to a **mock data/service layer** shaped like a future REST API (data contracts defined in SRS), so the real backend can replace it later without rewriting UI. | 2026-09-25 |
| D3 | All 26 modules in scope, including `DEV`-tagged, Pusat AI, and Killer Features — they are part of the system being cloned. Kept in Phase F (last) because nothing depends on them. | 2026-09-25 |
| D4 | AI features (M13, M25, parts of M06) are simulated with canned responses; payments (M19) simulated with fake gateway states. No external services. | 2026-09-25 |
| D6 | **UI must be responsive across devices** — mobile (≥320px), tablet, desktop, wide screens; no horizontal page scroll; admin sidebar becomes a drawer on small screens. Every module is verified at 375px, 768px, and 1280px in discovery, design, and E2E (3 Playwright projects). | 2026-09-25 |
| D7 | **Fidelity first.** The rebuild replicates prestasiplus.my exactly — every flow, epic, feature, function, field, button, message, and state. No new features and no "improvements". Deviations are allowed only for broken behaviour (404s, layout/responsive defects, contradictory rules needing one value) and each must be listed in the module's "Fidelity deviations" table and approved by the user. | 2026-09-25 |
| D8 | **Completeness is proven, not claimed.** Each module gets an automated element inventory of the live site plus a link crawl; URS maps every element to a Function; an independent coverage audit re-checks live vs. docs. Any unmapped element blocks G1. | 2026-09-25 |
| D9 | **Deviation rule (user, standing for all modules):** if the live site is broken or contradictory, the rebuild may fix/improvise it; if the live site works fine, replicate it exactly — no deviation. The Delivery Lead applies this rule directly; the user is not asked per item. "Broken" includes: 404/dead links, wrong labels/titles, visual bugs (e.g. active state not shown), layout/responsive breakage, controls unusable on touch devices, unreadable text, and contradictory rules. Working-but-improvable behaviour (e.g. accessibility enhancements) stays as live. | 2026-09-25 |
| D11 | **Large collections are deterministic virtual datasets** (Delivery Lead, resolves XL-02). Where live shows large totals (questions ~130k, videos 2,260, schools 10,220, users, sessions…), the mock repository reports the live total and generates rows on demand deterministically (stable ids in the live id range, attributes distributed to match the observed per-subject/per-level/per-status counts). Observed anchor records (live ids/texts seen in discovery) override generated rows at their ids; module fixtures (e.g. M05/M08 practice questions) are anchors inside the same dataset, not additions to the total. Every count on every screen is computed from the dataset, so there is one source and no contradictions; where live itself showed contradictory totals, the approved D9 deviation picks the value and the dataset is built to it. **Exception (XL-34):** a few observed display figures that no dataset definition reproduces (e.g. M06 hub 20,529 / 14,673) are declared per module as documented display constants — outside the fit, never shown next to a contradicting computed figure. | 2026-09-25 |
| D12 | **One owner per shared store.** Question store = M04 (incl. SPM pool, PKSK rows, structured bank); practice session + answer log + revision queue + mastery counters = M05; users/schools = M01/M02; curriculum = M03. Other modules read/write only through the owner's contract; no parallel schemas or virtual ids. | 2026-09-25 |
| D13 | **All agents use Opus 5.5** (user, 2026-09-25: "aku nak prototaip dibina guna opus 5.5"). Every role in `.claude/agents/` is set to `model: opus`; every delegation passes `model: opus`. | 2026-09-25 |
| D14 | **No payment-gateway or external-platform screens; simulate success / failed only** (user, 2026-09-27, at M19 G1: "payment gateway atau external platforms punya screen tak payah buat. tu nanti kita integrate saja. buat simulation success/failed je."). Applies to M19 payments, M20 WhatsApp redirect and any other external hand-off; real integration later. | 2026-09-27 |
| D15 | **Release 1.0 build priority** (user, 2026-09-27): "aku nak tengok dulu prototaip screens yang di bawah label 1.0" — the screens ticked in columns *Murid 1.0 / Guru 1.0 / Admin Sekolah 1.0* of `roles-matrix/Matriks-Akses-Peranan .xlsx` are built first, **all 3 roles, full gates** (user chose over student-first / quick visual). Priority only — the rest of live is still rebuilt later. The 3 undiscovered 1.0 screens (Heatmap Topik Lemah, DSKP Heatmap, Laporan Bulanan) are discovered now. | 2026-09-27 |
| D16 | **New module M27 "School Admin Portal"** owns the undocumented `/admin-sekolah/*` screens (10 of 17 Admin Sekolah 1.0 items + DSKP Heatmap, Laporan Bulanan). Module count 26 → 27; progress formula uses ÷ 27 from now. No school-admin/guru/murid test accounts exist: discover with the admin account only; anything not reachable is INFERRED/PROPOSED (user, 2026-09-27). | 2026-09-27 |
| D17 | **G2 for replicate-live modules** (M10, M11, M12, M16, M17, M18 and later): approved SRS (§2, §8, §10 375/768/1280 tables, deviation tables) + live discovery screenshots + design-system + shells count as the design spec; only shells.md v1.2 (D-R1) is new design work (user, 2026-09-27). | 2026-09-27 |
| D18 | **Model split** (user, 2026-09-30: "aku beri kau je guna Opus 5.5 sebagai ketua dan pengawal selia sahaja. apa2 task serahkan pada subagents dan guna Sonnet"): main session = Opus 5.5, lead and supervisor only; every task goes to a subagent on **Sonnet 5** (`model: sonnet`). Supersedes D13. CLAUDE.md §1 updated. | 2026-09-30 |
| D22 | **No generated media; source only** (user, 2026-10-02: "jangan jana apa2 video, gambar, atau audio. ambil dari sumber sahaja"). No generated video/image/audio files and no fabricated media ids/links (fake YouTube ids, invented thumbnails, synthetic audio). Media comes only from live prestasiplus.my or the source server; where none was observed, show what live shows for "no media". M09 placeholder `SEED…` video rows are being replaced with live data. Relayed to all sessions. | 2026-10-02 |
| D24 | **Server code/data is the authoritative source where live cannot be observed** (user, 2026-10-02: "akses je server dan rujuk kod/data"). For screens/behaviour our live account cannot reach (e.g. Admin Sekolah views behind 403), the server code and data (`/var/www/prestasiplus_cloud`, local copy `D:Dev Projectsprestasiplus-reference`) are the specification — tagged SERVER, not UNVERIFIED, and no school-admin account is requested. Live observation still wins where it exists. Privacy rule unchanged (no real names/personal data from server data into the repo). | 2026-10-02 |
| D23 | **L45 Jemput Ibu Bapa + avatar** (user, 2026-10-02: "asalkan capai requirements aku, proceed saja"): (a) L45 parts not observable without a murid account are built as PROPOSED from the reference rebuild, marked "Perlu pengesahan"; observed items (route guard, 403 copy, POST generate route, `/ibu-bapa/terima/{token}` + "Link Tidak Sah" copy) are replicated exactly. (b) Owner: M02 = student screen, invite tokens, parent↔student link; M01 = acceptance page `/ibu-bapa/terima/{token}` and parent registration/login hand-off. (c) Initials avatar keeps the shell SVG data URL (no external ui-avatars.com call, D4); M01 SRS Q8 closed. | 2026-10-02 |
| D25 | **Card download PNG allowed** (user, 2026-10-03: "yela.. 1 la", on M14 OQ-M14-20): "Muat Turun" on S15/S18 captures the user's own on-screen card with html2canvas and downloads a PNG, as live and SRS (US-M14-13/15, AC-13.2/15.4). Not "generated media" under D22: it is a client-side capture of real on-screen content, not fabricated or placeholder media. New dependency html2canvas (lazy-loaded). | 2026-10-03 |
| D26 | **M27 Part B G1 approved** (user, 2026-10-09: "setuju semua" on URS §17.1): G1-1 school admin activates/deactivates own-school teachers (Panduan), pupils read-only; G1-2 accounts created by a school admin are active at once; G1-3 S12 "Tambah Pelajar" + "Eksport CSV" on S6/S7 (SERVER); G1-4/5/6 S2 DSKP Heatmap, S5 Laporan Bulanan, S8 Kelas Sekolah (classroom only) built as PROPOSED; G1-7 breadcrumb "← Dashboard Sekolah" on S1–S3, S5–S8; G1-8 "Menyamar" adopted (needs XR-M27-21 from M01); G1-9 S12 password min 8. | 2026-10-09 |
| D27 | **M22 Tiket Bantuan G1 approved** (user via lead, 2026-10-09: "setuju" = both defaults): OQ-M22-01 admin queue is **read-only**, with no reply or status-change write path; OQ-M22-02 **Admin Sekolah cannot open the ticket queue** (`/admin/tiket` stays platform admin only). URS/SRS M22 v1.1, audit round 2 PASS. Row L04 → 40%. | 2026-10-09 |
| D28 | **v2 direction: new system, old ones untouched** (user, 2026-10-16, brainstorm only, no build yet): (a) build a new system and leave prestasiplus.my and prestasiplus.cloud alone; (b) frontend = this Vue prototype, backend = NestJS + PostgreSQL; (c) scope = all features of .my + .cloud, but question intake and question display are redesigned; anything not in prestasiplus.cloud is out of scope (e.g. contributor rewards); (d) two portals with strict separation (one route = one portal = one layout): Portal Pentadbiran `admin.*` for admin / admin_sekolah / pegawai_ppd, with access copied from the old .cloud panel (`role:admin,admin_sekolah,pegawai_ppd` + `pusat:` national-only gates), and Portal Pembelajaran for murid / guru / ibu bapa; admin student preview via an explicit preview/impersonation mode; (e) one canonical question bank and one intake pipeline (teacher PDF → OCR + AI extraction to the user's exam schema → tagging/dedupe → human review → publish); the user will share the exam schema in another session. Sequencing: finish and test the current prototype dev first; sample PDFs and the v2 G1 come later. Live checks confirmed the shell-switch complaint (admin menu → student shell on /spm/*, /battle*, /admin/audit-logs, /admin/duta-applications). | 2026-10-16 |
| D29 | **v2 prototype update rules** (user, 2026-10-16): (a) data will be migrated from prestasiplus.cloud, probably except exam-question data, which is reloaded from scratch; (b) update the prototype towards v2 now: where the flow/process is unchanged, replicate the old system, and where it changes (portals, question intake/display), update directly; (c) **prestasiplus.cloud is the primary reference** where .my and .cloud differ, and .my is used only for things not in .cloud; (d) new UI: design system = **Nuxt UI** with our own theme, so it must not look like stock Nuxt UI. The audience is the next generation (SPM 2027 candidates born ~2010, Gen Z/Alpha cusp; later cohorts Gen Alpha); (e) strictness: pages use only design-system components + theme tokens, and Tailwind utilities are allowed for layout only (flex/grid/gap/spacing), with no arbitrary colours/values; (f) light + dark mode. This supersedes the D7/D9/D10 "replicate, don't redesign" rules for UI/theme and for the changed flows. | 2026-10-16 |
| D30 | **v2: one Portal Pentadbiran** (user, 2026-10-16: "setuju"): the three old admin surfaces (`/admin` Blade, `admin.prestasiplus.cloud` panel, `/admin-filament`) merge into one Portal Pentadbiran with one URL, one login and one menu. Principles: one function = one place; the menu is grouped by job (Papan Pemuka, Pemantauan, Bank Soalan, Kertas & SPM, Kurikulum & Kandungan, Pengguna & Sekolah, Penglibatan, Kewangan, Sistem); the menu is filtered by role (Admin Pusat sees all; PPD/JPN see Pemantauan + Pengguna & Sekolah read-only, scoped to district/state; Admin Sekolah sees Papan Pemuka, Pemantauan, Pengguna & Sekolah and Kewangan, own school only), with no visible-but-forbidden items; the topbar always shows the role + scope; there is no jump to the student shell, only "Pratonton sebagai Pelajar" (new tab + banner); a role switcher appears only for dual-role users; Command Center becomes Pemantauan › Langsung with a full-screen mode, and the drill-down components are shared with the teacher variant. A Ctrl+K quick search is included (approved as part of this proposal). | 2026-10-16 |
| D31 | **v2 role answers** (user, 2026-10-16): (a) **PPD/JPN follow the old .cloud model** — single `pegawai_ppd` role, with scope derived as in .cloud (from the officer's school), because the old data is migrated; change it later only if needed. (b) **Dual role: allowed** (user: "personally boleh"). .cloud has a single `users.role` string column (no multi-role), so v2 stores a role list and migrates each old account as a one-item list. One active role at a time, with an explicit switcher. (c) **Admin "Mod Pelajar"**: admin may act fully in the student portal **as themselves (the admin account), not by impersonating a real student**, to test the student view. Entry and exit must be an explicit, visible role transition (switch + persistent banner + "Keluar Mod Pelajar"). Never a silent shell change. Data created in this mode is marked as test data. SPM Mock vs Simulasi is still open (explained to the user). | 2026-10-16 |

| D35 | **v2 two personalities + learner experience direction** (user, 2026-10-16): the user agreed with Versi B in principle but found it too colourless (all-grey sidebar icons), so a B+ variant is being prepared with colour on icons only. The user also said the theme alone feels like an ordinary system with nothing special for Gen Alpha. Decision: (a) **Portal Pentadbiran stays calm and professional** (B/B+); (b) **Portal Pembelajaran gets an "experience layer"** in the **"Gabungan"** style: premium dark-first visuals, game feel at key moments (micro-interactions, rolling numbers, animated streak, celebrations), **full-screen one-question-at-a-time** practice display (within the question-display redesign scope), and Cikgu Alesa as the character (feedback, empty states, wins); (c) Alesa artwork = **placeholder for now** (emoji/icon), with real character design later. This is presentation of existing features only, with no new features. Next: an interactive learner prototype (Home, Answer question, Win moment) for user review. | 2026-10-16 |

| D41 | **Bank Soalan v2: four more user-reported problems added to scope** (user, 2026-10-16): (1) several questions that refer to the **same diagram/stimulus** are not arranged well; (2) **answering subjective maths** is hard for students; (3) **entering teacher answer/mark schemes** is hard; (4) **matching of subjective answers** is inaccurate. Direction under design (pending user approval via a visual mockup): **stimulus groups** (one stimulus with ordered linked questions, kept together in every assembly, pinned display); **maths answer input** (step lines with a maths keyboard, a final-answer box per part, photo-of-working fallback); a **structured mark scheme** (marking points K/N/P with marks, accepted equivalents, tolerance, units, ECF; AI import from the teacher's scheme PDF); **equivalence-based auto-marking** with confidence and a teacher review queue. The user also asked to see the Bank Soalan design visually before approving decisions. | 2026-10-16 |

| D46 | **The exam schema is derived by us, not supplied by the user** (user, 2026-10-17: "DSKP semalam tu adalah panduan untuk hasilkan skema SPM dan kau boleh rujuk dalam DB .cloud untuk existing soalan"). (a) The v2 exam/question schema (question types, paper sections, parts, stimuli, mark-scheme format K/N/P, blueprints per subject×kertas, DSKP tagging) is **derived from the DSKP extracts + the existing questions in the .cloud database**, then proposed for user approval. Every "waiting for the user's schema" item (D28e, D39, D40, Q1/Q-B1) is resolved this way. (b) The user authorised **read-only SSH access to the .cloud server DB**: SELECT only on question/paper/blueprint/scheme tables; statistics and samples only, no full dumps; no user/student/report tables; DB credentials read on the server only and never copied; no changes to the server or data. | 2026-10-17 |

| D57 | **Security defaults for contracts (LEAD, delegated by the user 2026-10-19: "buat apa patut")**: (a) Every write route and every unauthenticated lookup documents 429 RATE_LIMITED with retryAfter and Retry-After. (b) Responses carrying credentials or tokens send Cache-Control: no-store. (c) GET /admin/config/defaults is Admin Pusat only, no-store; accounts created with a default password are must-change-password (as D55c). (d) GET /students/search is staff only (teacher, school_admin, admin), scoped to the caller's school for teacher and school_admin, with 429. (e) A caller can assign only roles at or below its own: school_admin assigns student or teacher in its own school; only admin assigns admin, ppd_officer or school_admin. (f) Parent invites: the token is random, hashed, single-use and expiring. GET /me/parent-invite issues a fresh token instead of returning a stored one. The public preview shows minimal child data (first name and school), is no-store and rate-limited. A parent-child link can be listed and removed by the student, the parent and the school admin (new unlink route, PDPA for minors). Accepting notifies the student and the school. The parent's e-mail is masked to the student. | 2026-10-19 |

| D81 | **Kertas Subjektif content method: upload → OCR → sunting → terbit** (user, 2026-10-30: "aku prefer upload -> sunting macam tulis biasa", then "upload -> OCR? -> sunting -> publish"). This supersedes the page-image options A, B and C in `v2-admin-student-pipeline.md` §10.1. Flow:
1. The admin uploads the paper PDF, plus the skema PDF if there is one.
2. OCR and extraction turn it into editable text, tables, cropped figures and maths, with question numbering kept.
3. The admin edits it like a normal document. The skema is edited in a separate tab.
4. Terbit. Students read the paper as reflowing text, not page images, and reveal "Tunjuk Skema Pemarkahan" as in .cloud.

Rules:
- The flow sits inside the existing Kertas Subjektif screens (D79d).
- It reuses the v2 intake PDF/page/crop capability.
- OCR must work for scanned PDFs, so the go-live setting `OCR_ENGINE=none` no longer holds for this flow.
- The OCR engine choice and its cost are a G2 decision for user approval.
- A digital PDF uses its text layer first. | 2026-10-30 |

| D89 | **Soalan by Chapter: the admin picks the Bab manually** (user, 2026-10-31: "Admin pilih bab sendiri").

Findings, checked read-only:
- V2 production: 0 of 408,197 approved MCQs have `chapter_id` or `topic_id`. The V2 Bab page lists chapters with "0 soalan", and its Mula leads to a flag-OFF Latih Tubi, so it is broken (D9).
- V3 UAT: 0 of 339,112 secondary questions have `chapter_id`, although 686 T4/T5 chapters exist.
- The local demo questions have no chapter links either (QA B-01, `docs/qa/r1-student-smoke-2026-10-31.md`).

Decision:
- The admin question form/Studio gets a required-when-relevant **"Bab"** select (chapters for the chosen subject + level), saved as `question_versions.chapter_id`.
- Soalan by Chapter serves the approved questions linked to a chapter.
- Legacy MCQs appear in Bab only after an admin sets their Bab.
- Chapters with no questions show a clear empty-state message.
- Not derived from DSKP; no data invented.
- Owner: prestasiplus-30 (R1). The chapter-page empty state stays with prestasiplus-8b's FE patch. | 2026-10-31 |
| D90 | **Shared stimulus = one group, never a standalone "question"** (user, 2026-10-31: "ada soalan yang mempunyai gambar dan 2 soalan merujuk pada gambar tu, V1 dan V2 jadikan gambar 1 soalan dan soalan pertama soalan asing dan soalan 2 soalan asing. sedangkan 2 soalan tu perlu rujuk gambar yang sama dan tak perlu asingkan gambar menjadi soalan"). This confirms D41 and is mandatory for V3. Where V1/V2 differ, this is a D9 fix, not replication.

(1) Model: a figure, passage or table that several questions refer to is ONE stimulus inside a stimulus group, with N member questions. The stimulus is never an item or question of its own and never carries marks.

(2) Authoring (Studio, intake):
- The admin can create a group: one stimulus plus 2 or more questions, add or remove members, and reorder them.
- Intake/import links one figure to EVERY question that refers to it ("Rajah 1 → S1–S2"), not only the first.

(3) Paper builder: placing a group places all its members, contiguous and in group order. A member can't be placed alone in a Bijak paper. Removing one member warns or removes the group. Jana Kertas treats a group as one unit.

(4) Student display:
- Bijak, Simulasi, Kuiz and practice show the stimulus once per group, with every member question below it: the "Lihat {label}" chip below 1024px, a pinned panel from 1024px (D83 D-3).
- In random drills (Latih Tubi/Databank) a member question may be served with its group stimulus shown above it, but the stimulus is never served as a question.

(5) The admin "Dilihat murid di" explains any exclusion. A silent drop of a group member is a defect. | 2026-10-31 |
| D80 | **Bijak SPM: KBAT encoding reconciled with v2-dskp-integration; question intake must be DSKP-aware** (2026-10-30, principle "tiru sistem lama dan berpandukan DSKP"; no new user question): (1) KBAT is the optional `kbat` tag of `v2-dskp-integration` decision A9 ('mengaplikasi' / 'menganalisis' / 'menilai' / 'mencipta' / none = Bukan KBAT), per question with an optional leaf-part override, independent of TP and **not a submit or publish gate**. This is consistent with D76 (the four levels are the DSKP's KBAT); the 1-to-6 "cognitive level" field is withdrawn. (2) Jana Kertas pool eligibility = unit or T1-T3 bucket, TP, marks, key, allowed mode and `tag_review = false`; the preview reason "tiada tahap kognitif" is replaced by "Perlu semakan tag". (3) The question upload must understand the DSKP: new stories US-41 (per-part unit, TP, KBAT in the intake tag editor, BJS-17) and US-42 (AI extractor receives the DSKP TP descriptors and KBAT definitions, suggests per-part tags with confidence, a human confirms per A6/A9, BJS-18). Both need the `v2-dskp-integration` branch (`origin/claude/determined-dijkstra-ju5q60`) merged. Architecture: `docs/architecture/v2-bijak-spm-standards.md` section 14 (R-1 to R-6). Requirements amended in `docs/requirements/v2-bijak-spm.md`. | 2026-10-30 |

## 0a. Roadmap to UI & prototype (user: "teruskan sampai dapat UI dan Prototype")

Two tracks run in parallel:
- **Requirements track (G1)** — M08–M26: discovery → URS/SRS → audit → G1, 3 browser lanes.
- **Build track (G2→G5)** — modules that passed G1:
  1. **XL** cross-module logic review of approved modules (flows connect, entities/APIs/seeds consistent, links resolve) → fixes routed to owners. First batch: M01–M07.
  2. **G2 foundation** — `solution-architect`: `docs/architecture/overview.md`, ADRs, `build-plan.md`; `ui-ux-designer`: `docs/design/design-system.md` (tokens, FA→Lucide map, components), `docs/design/shells.md`.
  3. **G3 foundation build** — scaffold Vue 3 + Vite + Tailwind + Lucide, shells, routing/guards, mock service layer + canonical seeds, shared components, test harness (Vitest + Playwright ×3 viewports).
  4. **Per module** (phase order): design spec → build (frontend-developer) → G4 review (code-reviewer ∥ security-engineer where relevant) → G5 QA against every AC on 375/768/1280 → module ✅.
  5. Provisional E2E (unobserved live states) stay tagged until the user approves live sessions / test accounts.

Build started on branch `feature/student-prototype`. **Build plan v1.2** (`docs/architecture/build-plan.md` §S): waves W0.1–W0.10 (foundation, up to 4 lanes) then W1–W9 (student modules). **First visible milestone (25 tasks):** F-01..06, 09..15, 17, 19, 21, 23..26, 29 + M01-T1, M01-T3, M05-T1, M05-T3 → log in as pelajar@demo.test → /dashboard. Critical path F-01→F-03→F-05→F-09→F-10→F-24→F-25→F-26→M05-T1→M05-T3. M04-T6b `/laporan-soalan` IN slice (Delivery Lead). F-29/F-30 + M08/M09 tasks wait for XL batch 2. **W0.1 F-01 ✅** (all green: lint, typecheck, 4 unit tests, build, Playwright smoke ×3). **W0.2:** F-04 API client ✅ (97 tests green; `api.raw` built → SQ-M13-01 resolvable) · F-02 tokens 🟡 · F-03 time/format 🟡 · F-01 code review 🟡. Doc follow-ups for architect (batch after W0.2): add M02 to slice registry text; Node ≥22.22.2 (jsdom 30); record `api.raw` exception + `responseType`; `api.list`/`api.multipart` helpers; array query encoding to confirm in backend phase. Student slice = M01 student flows, M05, M06 student, M03 /topics + /peta-belajar, M07 /pksk, M08 student screens, M09 reader screens, M04 report modal. Admin/teacher screens = next slice.

- **Queued cross-module items:** M01/M02 user ids → integer (M07 grant form takes a numeric id; M07 SQ-02). M04 store needs PKSK fields, excluded from M04 list/counts (M07 OQ-09).

### M01 — Authentication & Account
- [x] `/login` — Log in (email + password, remember me)

- [x] `/password/reset` — Forgot password (email)

- [x] `/logout` — GET link, no confirmation
- [x] `/profile` — My profile
- [x] `/profile/edit` — Edit profile & change password _(newly found)_
- [x] `/tetapan` — Personal settings
- [x] `/settings` — **404 on live site** (menu link is dead; drop or merge into `/tetapan`)
- [x] `/notifikasi` — Notifications inbox _(shared with M22)_
- [x] Role-based redirect after login (admin → `/admin`; intended URL ignored)
- [x] Global shells & design tokens → `docs/discovery/00-global-ui.md`

Discovery: `docs/discovery/M01-authentication.md` · screenshots `docs/discovery/img/M01/` (24), `img/global/` (9).

Notes:
- Not observable read-only: post-submit success/error screens for register, reset, IC reset, and profile saves; landing pages of non-admin roles.
- Live-site inconsistencies (don't copy; rebuild must pick one): min password 6 (register/reset) vs 8 (profile); IC max length 14 vs 20; question count 128,989 (login) vs 130,152 (admin); parents wrongly shown level picker; 1-2-3 stepper decorative only.
- Live stack: Laravel + Alpine.js + Tailwind v4 + Font Awesome 6.5.1; fonts Inter + Outfit; single breakpoint at 1024px. Responsive bugs to avoid: `/tetapan` h-scroll at 375, register cramped at 768, `/dashboard` 9px overflow at 768, notification filter strip h-scroll.
- Separate `/sponsor/register` exists → belongs to M21.

URS/SRS: `docs/requirements/urs/M01-authentication.md` · `docs/requirements/srs/M01-authentication.md` — v1.1: 3 epics, 16 features, 110 functions, 26 stories, 97 active ACs, 67 FRs, 21 mock endpoints, 49 E2E; coverage 934/934 elements (363 content + 571 shell). Old DN list withdrawn (D7).
Audit: `docs/qa/audit/M01-audit.md` — round 1 **FAIL** (0 Critical / 5 Major / 8 Minor; 56 claims checked, 9 wrong) → all 12 doc findings fixed in v1.1 → round 2 **PASS** (all closed, no regressions; AUD-M01-14 minor fixed by Delivery Lead). AUD-M01-13 fixed.
Deviations resolved by user rule D9: DV-01..08, 10–14 approved (fix); DV-15 rejected (replicate live); DV-09 withdrawn. Docs → v1.2 "Approved (G1)". Still pending: validation-probe permission (OQ-1) — post-submit copy stays INFERRED until then.

## 6. Per-module workflow (G1)

For each module, in order:
1. **DISC** — Discovery agent (read-only, Playwright):
   a. **Link crawl** from every module route: collect every internal link, form action, and detail/sub-route (e.g. `/x/{id}`, `/x/create`, `?tab=`) → any new route is added to the checklist here.
   b. **Element inventory** per screen via a script (not judgement): every link, button, input/select/textarea (+ options), checkbox/radio, tab, dropdown/menu item, modal/drawer (incl. hidden `x-show`/`display:none` ones), table (+ columns, row actions, filters, sort, pagination), card/widget, heading, badge, empty/error message, form (+ method/action). Saved as `docs/discovery/inventory/Mxx/<screen>.json` with stable IDs `EL-Mxx-<screen>-nnn`.
   c. Open every tab, dropdown, modal, accordion, and filter to reveal hidden UI (without submitting).
   d. Narrative doc `docs/discovery/Mxx-<slug>.md` + screenshots `docs/discovery/img/Mxx/`.
2. **URS** — `product-analyst` → `docs/requirements/urs/Mxx-<slug>.md`: Epic → Feature → Function breakdown, user stories, coverage matrix (every EL mapped), fidelity deviations.
3. **SRS** — `product-analyst` (functional) + `solution-architect` (data model, interfaces) → `docs/requirements/srs/Mxx-<slug>.md`: every Function specified.
4. **AUD** — an independent agent (not the author) re-walks the live screens against the inventory, URS, and SRS: finds unmapped elements, missing routes, wrong copy, missed states, and undocumented deviations. Gaps go back to step 1/2/3 until zero.
5. **G1** — User reviews URS + SRS + audit report → ✅ in the board.

Master indexes: `docs/requirements/URS.md` and `docs/requirements/SRS.md` link every module doc plus the shared sections (roles, glossary, global NFRs).

**Discovery rules (live production, admin account):**
- Read-only: open screens, forms, and modals; never click Save / Submit / Delete / Update / Approve / Send, or anything that changes data or triggers email/notifications.
- Credentials never written to any file.
- No real personal data in docs or screenshots — record structure only (column names, types, formats); blur or crop screenshots where needed.
- One browser session → discovery runs one module at a time (no parallel browser agents).
